{"parentUuid":"9f2ce548-0b2c-4726-a026-6d2d2acb4eca","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"e61e834d-815b-4b42-a7af-2d703ec1db4b","agentId":"a0ced397e656803cf","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01QwC3DzdGtu2EWJTK3XZyDp","type":"tool_result","content":"import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from \"next/server\";\n\nimport { stripe } from \"@/lib/clients/stripe\";\nimport { findAccountByKey, updateAccountKey } from \"@/src/lib/db-layer\";\nimport {\n  validateCreditPurchaseAmount,\n  computeCreditPurchase,\n} from \"./validation\";\n\n/**\n * Stripe Checkout API Route for Credit Purchases\n *\n * Creates a Stripe Checkout Session for HyperWhisper credit purchases. The\n * license key IS the wallet, so this is the only way to obtain a key: a guest\n * can buy credits with no key and the webhook mints + emails one. Topping up an\n * existing key is the other path.\n *\n * REQUEST BODY:\n * - amount: whole dollars, 5..500 (1000 credits per $1)\n * - licenseKey (optional): when present, top up that key instead of minting\n * - email (optional): mint path only. Prefills the Stripe checkout email so the\n *   minted key is emailed to that address; ignored when topping up a key.\n *\n * PRICING (two non-bundled line items):\n * - Credits: amount * 100 cents -> amount * 1000 credits\n * - Processing fee (6%): round(amount * 100 * 0.06) cents — revenue, never\n *   granted as credits, and non-refundable.\n *\n * STRIPE METADATA (read by the webhook):\n * - purchase_type: \"credits\"\n * - credit_amount: credits to grant\n * - fee_cents: the processing fee charged\n * - license_key: present ONLY when topping up an existing key (absent => mint)\n */\n\nexport async function POST(req: NextRequest) {\n  try {\n    const body = await req.json();\n    const { licenseKey, amount, email } = body as {\n      licenseKey?: unknown;\n      amount?: unknown;\n      email?: unknown;\n    };\n\n    // Validate amount: whole dollars within [MIN, MAX].\n    const amountError = validateCreditPurchaseAmount(amount);\n    if (amountError !== null) {\n      return NextResponse.json({ error: amountError }, { status: 400 });\n    }\n\n    const { creditAmount, creditCents, feeCents } = computeCreditPurchase(\n      amount as number\n    );\n\n    const siteUrl =\n      process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SITE_URL || \"https://hyperwhisper.com\";\n\n    // Optional license key: when supplied, this is a top-up of an existing\n    // wallet; when absent, the webhook mints a brand-new key on payment.\n    // Trim first: a whitespace-only key must NOT count as \"present\" (which would\n    // skip the mint path and then fail the key lookup). Normalize once and use the\n    // trimmed value everywhere below.\n    const normalizedLicenseKey =\n      typeof licenseKey === \"string\" && licenseKey.trim().length > 0\n        ? licenseKey.trim()\n        : undefined;\n    const hasLicenseKey = normalizedLicenseKey !== undefined;\n\n    // Mint path only: a guest-supplied email to prefill at checkout. Loose\n    // format check — Stripe re-validates and the buyer can still edit it. The\n    // webhook mints/emails the key off the email Stripe ultimately collects.\n    const guestEmail =\n      !hasLicenseKey &&\n      typeof email === \"string\" &&\n      /^[^\\s@]+@[^\\s@]+\\.[^\\s@]+$/.test(email.trim())\n        ? email.trim().toLowerCase()\n        : undefined;\n\n    const metadata: Record<string, string> = {\n      purchase_type: \"credits\",\n      credit_amount: creditAmount.toString(),\n      fee_cents: feeCents.toString(),\n    };\n\n    // Resolve the Stripe customer for the top-up path so credits attach to the\n    // same customer/email. The mint path lets Stripe collect the email itself.\n    let stripeCustomerId: string | undefined;\n\n    if (normalizedLicenseKey) {\n      const license = await findAccountByKey(normalizedLicenseKey);\n\n      if (!license) {\n        console.error(\n          \"License lookup failed for key:\",\n          normalizedLicenseKey.substring(0, 7)\n        );\n        return NextResponse.json(\n          { error: \"Invalid license key\" },\n          { status: 400 }\n        );\n      }\n\n      // Reject non-granted (e.g. revoked) licenses: credits added to them would\n      // be unusable because /api/license/credits refuses to validate or deduct\n      // them.\n      if (license.status !== \"granted\") {\n        return NextResponse.json(\n          { error: `License is ${license.status}` },\n          { status: 400 }\n        );\n      }\n\n      const email = license.email;\n      if (!email) {\n        return NextResponse.json(\n          { error: \"License has no email associated\" },\n          { status: 400 }\n        );\n      }\n\n      metadata.license_key = normalizedLicenseKey;\n\n      // Find or create the Stripe customer by email and cache it on the license.\n      stripeCustomerId = license.stripeCustomerId ?? undefined;\n      if (!stripeCustomerId) {\n        const existingCustomers = await stripe.customers.list({\n          email,\n          limit: 1,\n        });\n\n        if (existingCustomers.data.length > 0) {\n          stripeCustomerId = existingCustomers.data[0].id;\n        } else {\n          const newCustomer = await stripe.customers.create({\n            email,\n            metadata: { license_key: normalizedLicenseKey },\n          });\n          stripeCustomerId = newCustomer.id;\n        }\n\n        await updateAccountKey(license.id, { stripeCustomerId });\n      }\n    }\n\n    // Create Stripe Checkout Session\n    // @ts-expect-error - managed_payments is in private preview\n    const session = await stripe.checkout.sessions.create({\n      mode: \"payment\",\n      // Top-up: attach to the resolved customer. Mint: always create a customer\n      // during checkout, prefilling the guest email when one was supplied.\n      ...(stripeCustomerId\n        ? { customer: stripeCustomerId }\n        : {\n            customer_creation: \"always\",\n            ...(guestEmail ? { customer_email: guestEmail } : {}),\n          }),\n      line_items: [\n        {\n          price_data: {\n            currency: \"usd\",\n            product_data: {\n              name: \"HyperWhisper Cloud credits\",\n              description: `${creditAmount.toLocaleString()} credits`,\n              // Managed Payments requires every line item's product to carry a\n              // tax_code. txcd_10000000 = \"General - Electronically Supplied\n              // Services\", which fits prepaid credits for a cloud STT service.\n              tax_code: \"txcd_10000000\",\n            },\n            unit_amount: creditCents,\n          },\n          quantity: 1,\n        },\n        {\n          price_data: {\n            currency: \"usd\",\n            product_data: {\n              name: \"Cloud processing fee (6%)\",\n              description: \"Non-refundable HyperWhisper Cloud processing fees\",\n              // Same Managed Payments requirement: the fee line needs a tax_code.\n              tax_code: \"txcd_10000000\",\n            },\n            unit_amount: feeCents,\n          },\n          quantity: 1,\n        },\n      ],\n\n      // Allow coupon/promotion codes\n      allow_promotion_codes: true,\n\n      // Metadata for webhook processing\n      metadata,\n\n      // Managed Payments: Stripe handles tax, invoicing, and compliance\n      managed_payments: { enabled: true },\n\n      // Land on the public confirmation page (works for guests, who have no\n      // account yet). It only personalizes copy from `credits` — entitlement is\n      // always enforced server-side via the webhook-minted key.\n      success_url: `${siteUrl}/purchase-success?session_id={CHECKOUT_SESSION_ID}&credits=${creditAmount}`,\n      cancel_url: `${siteUrl}/credits`,\n    });\n\n    // Return checkout URL to client\n    if (session.url) {\n      return NextResponse.json({\n        checkoutUrl: session.url,\n      });\n    } else {\n      throw new Error(\"No checkout URL returned from Stripe\");\n    }\n  } catch (error) {\n    console.error(\"Credit checkout error:\", error);\n\n    return NextResponse.json(\n      {\n        error: \"Failed to create checkout session\",\n        details: error instanceof Error ? error.message : \"Unknown error\",\n      },\n      { status: 500 }\n    );\n  }\n}","is_error":false}]},"uuid":"18a0555b-0fbc-4e3b-b8c7-cc469f60ad92","timestamp":"2026-07-29T11:34:15.875Z","toolUseResult":{"stdout":"import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from \"next/server\";\n\nimport { stripe } from \"@/lib/clients/stripe\";\nimport { findAccountByKey, updateAccountKey } from \"@/src/lib/db-layer\";\nimport {\n  validateCreditPurchaseAmount,\n  computeCreditPurchase,\n} from \"./validation\";\n\n/**\n * Stripe Checkout API Route for Credit Purchases\n *\n * Creates a Stripe Checkout Session for HyperWhisper credit purchases. The\n * license key IS the wallet, so this is the only way to obtain a key: a guest\n * can buy credits with no key and the webhook mints + emails one. Topping up an\n * existing key is the other path.\n *\n * REQUEST BODY:\n * - amount: whole dollars, 5..500 (1000 credits per $1)\n * - licenseKey (optional): when present, top up that key instead of minting\n * - email (optional): mint path only. Prefills the Stripe checkout email so the\n *   minted key is emailed to that address; ignored when topping up a key.\n *\n * PRICING (two non-bundled line items):\n * - Credits: amount * 100 cents -> amount * 1000 credits\n * - Processing fee (6%): round(amount * 100 * 0.06) cents — revenue, never\n *   granted as credits, and non-refundable.\n *\n * STRIPE METADATA (read by the webhook):\n * - purchase_type: \"credits\"\n * - credit_amount: credits to grant\n * - fee_cents: the processing fee charged\n * - license_key: present ONLY when topping up an existing key (absent => mint)\n */\n\nexport async function POST(req: NextRequest) {\n  try {\n    const body = await req.json();\n    const { licenseKey, amount, email } = body as {\n      licenseKey?: unknown;\n      amount?: unknown;\n      email?: unknown;\n    };\n\n    // Validate amount: whole dollars within [MIN, MAX].\n    const amountError = validateCreditPurchaseAmount(amount);\n    if (amountError !== null) {\n      return NextResponse.json({ error: amountError }, { status: 400 });\n    }\n\n    const { creditAmount, creditCents, feeCents } = computeCreditPurchase(\n      amount as number\n    );\n\n    const siteUrl =\n      process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SITE_URL || \"https://hyperwhisper.com\";\n\n    // Optional license key: when supplied, this is a top-up of an existing\n    // wallet; when absent, the webhook mints a brand-new key on payment.\n    // Trim first: a whitespace-only key must NOT count as \"present\" (which would\n    // skip the mint path and then fail the key lookup). Normalize once and use the\n    // trimmed value everywhere below.\n    const normalizedLicenseKey =\n      typeof licenseKey === \"string\" && licenseKey.trim().length > 0\n        ? licenseKey.trim()\n        : undefined;\n    const hasLicenseKey = normalizedLicenseKey !== undefined;\n\n    // Mint path only: a guest-supplied email to prefill at checkout. Loose\n    // format check — Stripe re-validates and the buyer can still edit it. The\n    // webhook mints/emails the key off the email Stripe ultimately collects.\n    const guestEmail =\n      !hasLicenseKey &&\n      typeof email === \"string\" &&\n      /^[^\\s@]+@[^\\s@]+\\.[^\\s@]+$/.test(email.trim())\n        ? email.trim().toLowerCase()\n        : undefined;\n\n    const metadata: Record<string, string> = {\n      purchase_type: \"credits\",\n      credit_amount: creditAmount.toString(),\n      fee_cents: feeCents.toString(),\n    };\n\n    // Resolve the Stripe customer for the top-up path so credits attach to the\n    // same customer/email. The mint path lets Stripe collect the email itself.\n    let stripeCustomerId: string | undefined;\n\n    if (normalizedLicenseKey) {\n      const license = await findAccountByKey(normalizedLicenseKey);\n\n      if (!license) {\n        console.error(\n          \"License lookup failed for key:\",\n          normalizedLicenseKey.substring(0, 7)\n        );\n        return NextResponse.json(\n          { error: \"Invalid license key\" },\n          { status: 400 }\n        );\n      }\n\n      // Reject non-granted (e.g. revoked) licenses: credits added to them would\n      // be unusable because /api/license/credits refuses to validate or deduct\n      // them.\n      if (license.status !== \"granted\") {\n        return NextResponse.json(\n          { error: `License is ${license.status}` },\n          { status: 400 }\n        );\n      }\n\n      const email = license.email;\n      if (!email) {\n        return NextResponse.json(\n          { error: \"License has no email associated\" },\n          { status: 400 }\n        );\n      }\n\n      metadata.license_key = normalizedLicenseKey;\n\n      // Find or create the Stripe customer by email and cache it on the license.\n      stripeCustomerId = license.stripeCustomerId ?? undefined;\n      if (!stripeCustomerId) {\n        const existingCustomers = await stripe.customers.list({\n          email,\n          limit: 1,\n        });\n\n        if (existingCustomers.data.length > 0) {\n          stripeCustomerId = existingCustomers.data[0].id;\n        } else {\n          const newCustomer = await stripe.customers.create({\n            email,\n            metadata: { license_key: normalizedLicenseKey },\n          });\n          stripeCustomerId = newCustomer.id;\n        }\n\n        await updateAccountKey(license.id, { stripeCustomerId });\n      }\n    }\n\n    // Create Stripe Checkout Session\n    // @ts-expect-error - managed_payments is in private preview\n    const session = await stripe.checkout.sessions.create({\n      mode: \"payment\",\n      // Top-up: attach to the resolved customer. Mint: always create a customer\n      // during checkout, prefilling the guest email when one was supplied.\n      ...(stripeCustomerId\n        ? { customer: stripeCustomerId }\n        : {\n            customer_creation: \"always\",\n            ...(guestEmail ? { customer_email: guestEmail } : {}),\n          }),\n      line_items: [\n        {\n          price_data: {\n            currency: \"usd\",\n            product_data: {\n              name: \"HyperWhisper Cloud credits\",\n              description: `${creditAmount.toLocaleString()} credits`,\n              // Managed Payments requires every line item's product to carry a\n              // tax_code. txcd_10000000 = \"General - Electronically Supplied\n              // Services\", which fits prepaid credits for a cloud STT service.\n              tax_code: \"txcd_10000000\",\n            },\n            unit_amount: creditCents,\n          },\n          quantity: 1,\n        },\n        {\n          price_data: {\n            currency: \"usd\",\n            product_data: {\n              name: \"Cloud processing fee (6%)\",\n              description: \"Non-refundable HyperWhisper Cloud processing fees\",\n              // Same Managed Payments requirement: the fee line needs a tax_code.\n              tax_code: \"txcd_10000000\",\n            },\n            unit_amount: feeCents,\n          },\n          quantity: 1,\n        },\n      ],\n\n      // Allow coupon/promotion codes\n      allow_promotion_codes: true,\n\n      // Metadata for webhook processing\n      metadata,\n\n      // Managed Payments: Stripe handles tax, invoicing, and compliance\n      managed_payments: { enabled: true },\n\n      // Land on the public confirmation page (works for guests, who have no\n      // account yet). It only personalizes copy from `credits` — entitlement is\n      // always enforced server-side via the webhook-minted key.\n      success_url: `${siteUrl}/purchase-success?session_id={CHECKOUT_SESSION_ID}&credits=${creditAmount}`,\n      cancel_url: `${siteUrl}/credits`,\n    });\n\n    // Return checkout URL to client\n    if (session.url) {\n      return NextResponse.json({\n        checkoutUrl: session.url,\n      });\n    } else {\n      throw new Error(\"No checkout URL returned from Stripe\");\n    }\n  } catch (error) {\n    console.error(\"Credit checkout error:\", error);\n\n    return NextResponse.json(\n      {\n        error: \"Failed to create checkout session\",\n        details: error instanceof Error ? error.message : \"Unknown error\",\n      },\n      { status: 500 }\n    );\n  }\n}","stderr":"","interrupted":false,"isImage":false,"noOutputExpected":false},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"9f2ce548-0b2c-4726-a026-6d2d2acb4eca","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/ray/Desktop/ray-os","sessionId":"0ad0c358-bd4c-4ff8-872f-2f81b1b9e66c","version":"2.1.220","gitBranch":"main","slug":"lively-giggling-hammock"}

Here is what I found.\n\n## `isValidKeyFormat` — what it checks\n\n`/Users/ray/Desktop/hyperwhisper-public/nextjs/lib/services/license-key.ts:99-111`. Two guards:\n\n```ts\nif (!key || typeof key !== \"string\") { return false; }\nconst pattern = /^HW-[A-HJ-NP-Z2-9]{4}-[A-HJ-NP-Z2-9]{4}-[A-HJ-NP-Z2-9]{4}-[A-HJ-NP-Z2-9]{4}$/;\nreturn pattern.test(key.toUpperCase());\n```\n\nIt rejects **non-strings** and anything not exactly 19 chars in `HW-XXXX-XXXX-XXXX-XXXX` shape. That first line — the `typeof` guard — is the load-bearing one here.\n\n**It is not dead**: `generateLicenseKey` calls it at line 74 as a self-check on freshly minted keys. But **no untrusted-input path calls it**. Git archaeology is unavailable (squashed release commit `e3708d4`; the only other hit is the generic dead-code sweep `0fec986`), so I moved on.\n\n## Untrusted entry points and their guards\n\n| Entry point | key source | type guard? | format guard? |\n|---|---|---|---|\n| `nextjs/app/api/license/validate/route.ts:89` | JSON body | **no** (`if (!license_key)` only) | no |\n| `nextjs/app/api/license/activate/route.ts:51` | JSON body | **no** | no |\n| `nextjs/app/api/license/credits/route.ts:83` | JSON body | yes (`typeof !== \"string\"`) | no |\n| `nextjs/app/api/license/credits/route.ts:33` | query param | n/a (always string) | no |\n| `nextjs/src/lib/auth-license-key-plugin.ts:26` | JSON body | yes (`z.string()`) | no |\n| `hyperwhisper-cloud/src/routes/post-process.ts:110` | **JSON body** | **no** (`c.req.json()` cast, unvalidated) | no |\n| cloud transcribe / usage / ws-streaming | query params | n/a | no |\n\nTwo of the \"no format guard\" outcomes are benign: `account_keys.key` carries `uniqueIndex(\"idx_account_keys_key\")` (`nextjs/src/db/schema/account-keys.ts`), so an unknown key is an indexed miss, not a table scan; and Drizzle parameterises, so no injection. The *type* gap is where the real damage is.\n\n---\n\nCANDIDATE finding\nfile: /Users/ray/Desktop/hyperwhisper-public/hyperwhisper-cloud/src/middleware/auth.ts\nline: 25\nsummary: `license_key` arriving as a non-string JSON value is never type-checked before `.slice()`/`.trim()` are called on it, so a malformed key produces an unhandled `TypeError` → opaque HTTP 500 instead of the intended 401 invalid-license response — exactly the case `isValidKeyFormat`'s `typeof key !== \"string\"` guard exists to reject.\nfailure_scenario: `POST /post-process` with `{\"text\":\"hi\",\"prompt\":\"fix\",\"license_key\":123}` reaches `validateAuth`; `!licenseKey` is false, so `maskLicenseKey(123)` runs `(123).length <= 8` → false → `(123).slice(0,4)` → `TypeError: key.slice is not a function` (reproduced locally). Hono's `app.onError` (`hyperwhisper-cloud/src/index.ts:67`) returns a 500 with an `error_id`. Sending an **array** instead (`\"license_key\":[\"a\",\"b\"]`, length ≤ 8) slips past `maskLicenseKey`, is forwarded verbatim to `https://www.hyperwhisper.com/api/license/validate`, and detonates on the Next.js side instead: `checkLicenseKey` calls `licenseKey.trim()` (`nextjs/src/lib/license-validation.ts:126`) → `TypeError` → the route's catch returns 500. That 500 is then classified transient by `auth.ts:70`, so it is **never negatively cached** — every retry costs a fresh 10s-budget HTTPS round-trip to the licensing API and burns one slot of the shared rate-limit bucket (see next finding).\ntrigger_path: attacker/buggy client → `POST /post-process` → `post-process.ts:110` `validateAuth({licenseKey: body.license_key})` → `auth.ts:31` → `auth.ts:25` `key.slice` → `index.ts:67` onError → 500 ‖ array variant → `auth.ts:36` fetch → `nextjs/app/api/license/validate/route.ts:98` → `license-validation.ts:126` `.trim()` → 500\noracle: [D1]\n\nCANDIDATE finding\nfile: /Users/ray/Desktop/hyperwhisper-public/hyperwhisper-cloud/src/middleware/auth.ts\nline: 70\nsummary: The 5xx-is-transient guard treats every non-5xx response as a definitive licence verdict, so an HTTP **429** from the licensing API is cached as `isValid: false` for a full hour — locking paying customers out of Cloud entirely.\nfailure_scenario: `validateLicenseViaApi` sends no `x-forwarded-for`, so `/api/license/validate` sees the Fly machine's egress IP via `getClientIPFromHeaders` and every Cloud user shares **one** `licenseValidateRateLimiter` bucket of 30 req/min (`nextjs/lib/rate-limit.ts`). Once that trips — a TTL-expiry thundering herd, 30 cache-missing users in one minute, or an attacker driving the un-negatively-cached malformed-key loop from the finding above — the route returns `429 {valid:false}`. `if (response.status >= 500)` is false, so control falls through to `cacheLicense(licenseKey, {isValid:false, credits:0})` at line 75 with `LICENSE_CACHE_TTL_SECONDS = 3600`. A paying user's real, granted licence is now pinned invalid for **1 hour**; `transcribe`, `post-process`, `assistant`, and `ws-streaming-deepgram` all call `validateAuth` with `forceRefresh=false`, so every one of them reads the poisoned entry and returns `invalidLicenseResponse()`. This is precisely the outcome the author's own comment at line 61 says must not happen (\"caching it would lock a paying user out for the full LICENSE_CACHE_TTL_SECONDS\") — 5xx was handled, 429 was missed.\ntrigger_path: burst or malformed-key flood → `/api/license/validate` rate limiter (shared Fly egress IP) → 429 → `auth.ts:70` guard passes → `auth.ts:75` `cacheLicense({isValid:false})` for 3600s → victim's next `POST /transcribe` → `auth.ts:112` cache HIT `isValid:false` → 401\noracle: [D1]\n\n---\n\n## Lighter pass on the other three\n\n- **`getAllAccountKeysForAdmin`** (`nextjs/src/lib/db-layer.ts:194`) — **false positive**, used intra-file at line 773 by `getAllAccountKeysWithCreditsForAdmin`, which is imported and called by `nextjs/server/api/routers/admin/customers.ts:55`. The admin licence-list capability is wired up and working; nothing is silently missing and no ad-hoc query replaces it.\n- **`createServerCaller`** (`nextjs/lib/trpc/server.ts:38`) — **genuinely dead**; its only other occurrences in the repo are inside its own JSDoc usage example. Nothing breaks: server-side code reaches data through `db-layer` directly rather than through a tRPC caller. Pure hygiene.\n- **`tryExtractCorrectionText`** (`hyperwhisper-cloud/src/lib/text-processing.ts:19`) — **false positive**, a mutually-recursive helper called seven times inside its own file (lines 44, 52, 61, 66, 106, 120) and by the exported wrapper `extractCorrectedText` at line 142, which is what external callers use."}],"stop_reason":"end_turn","stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":1694,"cache_read_input_tokens":74877,"output_tokens":3679,"server_tool_use":{"web_search_requests":0,"web_fetch_requests":0},"service_tier":"standard","cache_creation":{"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":1694},"inference_geo":"not_available","iterations":[{"input_tokens":2,"output_tokens":3679,"cache_read_input_tokens":74877,"cache_creation_input_tokens":1694,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":1694,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"type":"message"}],"speed":"standard"},"diagnostics":null},"requestId":"req_011CdWHDeGayTbzZRdqgbZ5d","attributionAgent":"general-purpose","type":"assistant","uuid":"7faa4578-fe56-44b1-89f0-96a29d3a4961","timestamp":"2026-07-29T11:37:12.231Z","effort":"high","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/ray/Desktop/ray-os","sessionId":"0ad0c358-bd4c-4ff8-872f-2f81b1b9e66c","version":"2.1.220","gitBranch":"main","slug":"lively-giggling-hammock"}

{"parentUuid":"2b4a3ae8-d4af-4be8-ad11-f169eb2a6fc9","isSidechain":true,"agentId":"a0ced397e656803cf","message":{"model":"claude-opus-5","id":"msg_011CdWHwyxBXgmWXTdG58ZpG","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_012ZutUe8CeK14t2SrFnLRC8","name":"SendMessage","input":{"to":"oracle-hunter","summary":"D1 final: 2 CANDIDATE findings + 3 benign symbols","message":"Lead [D1] complete. Final blocks, verbatim.\n\nCONTEXT (one paragraph, then the blocks):\n`isValidKeyFormat` (nextjs/lib/services/license-key.ts:99-111) is NOT dead — `generateLicenseKey` calls it at line 74 as a self-check on freshly minted keys. But no untrusted-input path calls it. Its first line is the load-bearing guard: `if (!key || typeof key !== \"string\") { return false; }`. Git archaeology unavailable (squashed release commit e3708d4; only other hit is generic dead-code sweep 0fec986).\n\nEntry-point audit — untrusted `licenseKey` sources and their guards:\n- nextjs/app/api/license/validate/route.ts:89 — JSON body — NO type guard (`if (!license_key)` only), no format guard\n- nextjs/app/api/license/activate/route.ts:51 — JSON body — NO type guard, no format guard\n- nextjs/app/api/license/credits/route.ts:83 — JSON body — HAS `typeof !== \"string\"`, no format guard\n- nextjs/app/api/license/credits/route.ts:33 — query param — always string\n- nextjs/src/lib/auth-license-key-plugin.ts:26 — JSON body — HAS `z.string()`\n- hyperwhisper-cloud/src/routes/post-process.ts:110 — JSON body — NO type guard (`c.req.json()` cast, unvalidated)\n- cloud transcribe/usage/ws-streaming — query params — always string\n\nTwo \"no format guard\" outcomes are benign: `account_keys.key` carries `uniqueIndex(\"idx_account_keys_key\")` (nextjs/src/db/schema/account-keys.ts), so an unknown key is an indexed miss, not a table scan; Drizzle parameterises, so no injection surface. The *type* gap is where the damage is.\n\n---\n\nCANDIDATE finding\nfile: /Users/ray/Desktop/hyperwhisper-public/hyperwhisper-cloud/src/middleware/auth.ts\nline: 25\nsummary: `license_key` arriving as a non-string JSON value is never type-checked before `.slice()`/`.trim()` are called on it, so a malformed key produces an unhandled `TypeError` -> opaque HTTP 500 instead of the intended 401 invalid-license response — exactly the case `isValidKeyFormat`'s `typeof key !== \"string\"` guard exists to reject.\nfailure_scenario: `POST /post-process` with `{\"text\":\"hi\",\"prompt\":\"fix\",\"license_key\":123}` reaches `validateAuth`; `!licenseKey` is false, so `maskLicenseKey(123)` runs `(123).length <= 8` -> false -> `(123).slice(0,4)` -> `TypeError: key.slice is not a function` (reproduced locally). Hono's `app.onError` (hyperwhisper-cloud/src/index.ts:67) returns a 500 with an `error_id`. Sending an array instead (`\"license_key\":[\"a\",\"b\"]`, length <= 8) slips past `maskLicenseKey`, is forwarded verbatim to `https://www.hyperwhisper.com/api/license/validate`, and detonates on the Next.js side instead: `checkLicenseKey` calls `licenseKey.trim()` (nextjs/src/lib/license-validation.ts:126) -> `TypeError` -> the route's catch returns 500. That 500 is then classified transient by auth.ts:70, so it is never negatively cached — every retry costs a fresh 10s-budget HTTPS round-trip to the licensing API and burns one slot of the shared rate-limit bucket (see next finding).\ntrigger_path: attacker/buggy client -> `POST /post-process` -> post-process.ts:110 `validateAuth({licenseKey: body.license_key})` -> auth.ts:31 -> auth.ts:25 `key.slice` -> index.ts:67 onError -> 500 || array variant -> auth.ts:36 fetch -> nextjs/app/api/license/validate/route.ts:98 -> license-validation.ts:126 `.trim()` -> 500\noracle: [D1]\n\nCANDIDATE finding\nfile: /Users/ray/Desktop/hyperwhisper-public/hyperwhisper-cloud/src/middleware/auth.ts\nline: 70\nsummary: The 5xx-is-transient guard treats every non-5xx response as a definitive licence verdict, so an HTTP 429 from the licensing API is cached as `isValid: false` for a full hour — locking paying customers out of Cloud entirely.\nfailure_scenario: `validateLicenseViaApi` sends no `x-forwarded-for`, so `/api/license/validate` sees the Fly machine's egress IP via `getClientIPFromHeaders` and every Cloud user shares ONE `licenseValidateRateLimiter` bucket of 30 req/min (nextjs/lib/rate-limit.ts). Once that trips — a TTL-expiry thundering herd, 30 cache-missing users in one minute, or an attacker driving the un-negatively-cached malformed-key loop from the finding above — the route returns `429 {valid:false}`. `if (response.status >= 500)` is false, so control falls through to `cacheLicense(licenseKey, {isValid:false, credits:0})` at line 75 with `LICENSE_CACHE_TTL_SECONDS = 3600`. A paying user's real, granted licence is now pinned invalid for 1 hour; `transcribe`, `post-process`, `assistant`, and `ws-streaming-deepgram` all call `validateAuth` with `forceRefresh=false`, so every one of them reads the poisoned entry and returns `invalidLicenseResponse()`. This is precisely the outcome the author's own comment at line 61 says must not happen (\"caching it would lock a paying user out for the full LICENSE_CACHE_TTL_SECONDS\") — 5xx was handled, 429 was missed.\ntrigger_path: burst or malformed-key flood -> `/api/license/validate` rate limiter (shared Fly egress IP) -> 429 -> auth.ts:70 guard passes -> auth.ts:75 `cacheLicense({isValid:false})` for 3600s -> victim's next `POST /transcribe` -> auth.ts:112 cache HIT `isValid:false` -> 401\noracle: [D1]\n\nBENIGN (other three zero-importer symbols):\n- `getAllAccountKeysForAdmin` (nextjs/src/lib/db-layer.ts:194) — false positive; called intra-file at line 773 by `getAllAccountKeysWithCreditsForAdmin`, which is imported and called by nextjs/server/api/routers/admin/customers.ts:55. The admin licence-list capability is wired up; nothing silently missing, no ad-hoc query replacing it.\n- `createServerCaller` (nextjs/lib/trpc/server.ts:38) — genuinely dead; its only other occurrences are inside its own JSDoc usage example. Nothing breaks: server-side code reaches data through `db-layer` directly rather than through a tRPC caller. Pure hygiene.\n- `tryExtractCorrectionText` (hyperwhisper-cloud/src/lib/text-processing.ts:19) — false positive; a mutually-recursive helper called seven times inside its own file (lines 44, 52, 61, 66, 106, 120) and by the exported wrapper `extractCorrectedText` at line 142, which is what external callers use.\n\nVerification notes: TypeError behaviour reproduced with a local pure-function harness (no DB/network); working tree untouched (read-only throughout); no secrets echoed.","type":"message","recipient":"oracle-hunter","content":"Lead [D1] complete. Final blocks, verbatim.\n\nCONTEX…"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":"tool_use","stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":3707,"cache_read_input_tokens":78244,"output_tokens":2745,"server_tool_use":{"web_search_requests":0,"web_fetch_requests":0},"service_tier":"standard","cache_creation":{"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":3707},"inference_geo":"not_available","iterations":[{"input_tokens":2,"output_tokens":2745,"cache_read_input_tokens":78244,"cache_creation_input_tokens":3707,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":3707,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"type":"message"}],"speed":"standard"},"diagnostics":null},"requestId":"req_011CdWHwxa6DT1Mte89LUKgX","attributionAgent":"general-purpose","type":"assistant","uuid":"6f7da3a9-492c-4a57-b60d-bfde16ea26cb","timestamp":"2026-07-29T11:46:27.379Z","effort":"high","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/ray/Desktop/ray-os","sessionId":"0ad0c358-bd4c-4ff8-872f-2f81b1b9e66c","version":"2.1.220","gitBranch":"main","slug":"lively-giggling-hammock"}
